| dv/auto_dv/evidence/gen_tdd_logs/lockstep/gen_fu_l48_local_runner_refusal.log | landing 48: gen_tb_local.sh refuses a cocotb-config outside the clone's pinned venv, a VPI library outside it, or an empty --libpython, and only then exports LIBPYTHON_LOC. A CORRECTNESS fix: the RED is the COMMITTED runner producing vcs_simv (1524544 bytes, vcs exit 0, no error) whose own command line carries the Python 3.9 site cocotb VPI library, in the shape where LIBPYTHON_LOC is UNSET. Records that the variable has THREE states and that measuring two of them produced two wrong reports: absent and empty behave OPPOSITELY, since ci/env.sh's guard assigns an empty string when the tool is present and assigns nothing when it is absent, which is a fresh worktree's shape. States what it does NOT claim (the run path is a bare assignment that already refuses, so this is a silent BUILD not a silent RUN) and why the library-path prepend is deliberately absent (measured inert). | dv/auto_dv/evidence/gen_tdd_logs/fcov/gen_fu_mret_prestate_corrigendum.log | landing 49: ONE corrigendum to TWO retained logs, gen_fu_l46_irq_step1b.log (md5 e9be074c...) and gen_fu_l47_irq_mret_bin.log (md5 1456cef8...), both UNCHANGED. Raised by rev56. The classifier read a retirement's pre-state MIE from the previous retired record, which is wrong for a handler's first instruction because a trap clears MIE and retires no record, so the previous record is the INTERRUPTED instruction whose MIE was necessarily set. Every mret in every one of our programs is a handler's first instruction, so the misread was total. The two logs' urg figures are accurate; their INTERPRETATION is inverted - the coverage landing 46 calls false was real, and the coverage landing 47 calls honest was the misclassification.
